- `c9c8311abd64` trap **tool.render_gate.owner_tree_resolution** = "trap" (state) {tool=render_gate.sh} w=4/5 — rev.cfg IS PER-TREE, UNCOMMITTED, SHARED STATE, and every committed byte-pin in this project is a 1920x1080 dummy-mode hash. The owner's main tree carries his window size (1384x768), so any DEMO recipe that takes its resolution from the tree renders at the wrong size there and every pin it is compared against goes red for a reason that has nothing to do with the binary. WHAT CHANGED (2026-08-30, fc70f26e): the two GATE SCRIPTS are now immune — tools/render_gate.sh and tools/warm_gate.sh pass --force_xres=1920 --force_yres=1080 on every row and never read Runtime/rev.cfg, verified ALL PASS in a tree whose rev.cfg says 1384x768 (48db47fafc50). THE OLD ADVICE IS DEAD: do NOT copy a binary into a stock-cfg worktree to check a gate any more, and still never touch his rev.cfg. WHAT IS STILL EXPOSED: every other DEMO invocation that does not pass the flags — tools/flip_rate.sh, tools/refrender_battery.sh, tools/ssao_gtao_battery.sh and any hand-typed ./DEMO --snapshot=... . For those, ADD --force_xres=1920 --force_yres=1080 to the command; that is now the remedy, and it works in any tree. Symptom to recognise: several pins failing at once with md5s that are unrelated to each other and to anything on file — check the PPM header (P6 <W> <H>) before hunting a regression.  (+1 superseded)

### trap
- `609d12df45a9` trap **trap.miplevel_minsize_frozen_static** = 5242.88 (count) {metric=mip_minsize pose=camA_t5965 scene=greets tool=m5} w=3/5  [floating] — MiplevelClipper (FDS/FRUSTRUM/FRUSTRUM.CPP) computes its small-face threshold as `static const float MinSize = XRes*YRes * 0.02;` - a FUNCTION-LOCAL static, initialised ONCE by whichever thread calls first, from whatever the XRes/YRes globals held then. MEASURED at greets cam A on a 1920x1080 run: MinSize = 5242.88, i.e. 512x512 - an offscreen bake got there first, so the MAIN view runs the whole process with a threshold 7.9x too small (it should be 41472). The source comment ("update if XRes and/or YRes changes...") documents the bug and nothing does it. Consequences: the poly-split path is entered for faces meant to take the uniform-mip fast path, the value silently depends on WHICH pass touches the clipper first (a scheduling property), and a window resize never updates it. NOT the cause of the M5 divergence - both branches still rasterise the face, verified - but a live resolution-dependence trap for anyone reading mip behaviour or pricing the split path.

Match executables (ps -Ao comm=), not argv. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from revival-fog]
- `5fc35421cbaf` trap **zsh.batch_script.silent_no_op** = "trap" (exact) {project=revival-fog shell=zsh tool=zsh} w=2/5  [floating]  ‹global› — Two zsh idioms turn a batch script into a silent no-op. (1) rm -f a/*.x b/*.y aborts on NOMATCH before deleting ANYTHING when one pattern matches nothing, so a self-cleaning run keeps every file - setopt local_options null_glob. (2) An option string expanded as ${OPT} instead of ${=OPT} is passed as ONE argv word, so --bare /path reaches argparse as a single unrecognised argument and every invocation fails. Both were found only because the output tree was 1681 MiB instead of 250 and every score file held a usage message, not because anything errored.

## Laws and traps without supporting records (44)
Flagged, not shouted: they cite a document, not a measurement. Add `depends_on` naming the records they rest on; they move to Attention only when something current depends on them.
- `804dba296145` law **detector.valid** = "valid" (state) {defect=wall detector=refdiff scene=greets} w=5/5 — refdiff (offline plane+heightfield reference, --refplane_dump + tools/refdiff_detect.py) is the validated wall-defect detector: exact 0.00 on the bare walls, fires on every era he labeled broken, orders them as he ordered them.
- `607c7cd2c810` trap **greets.displace.ref.fp_contract_backface** = "trap" (snapshot_md5) {flag=cull_front granularity=binary scene=greets tool=zsh} w=4/5 — Adding --cull_front as a TERNARY over two copies of the backface dot product moved BOTH greets pins WITH THE FLAG OFF: under -ffp-contract=fast the duplicated expression contracts differently and flips faces sitting exactly on the plane. Written as (test) != cullFront, leaving the float expression textually unchanged, all six pins are identical. Any flag that branches around a float comparison must not restate the comparison.
- `60e3e63bed65` trap **greets.displace.ref.smooth_seam_margin** = "trap" (state) {defect=wall flag=greets_displace_ref_margin scene=greets tool=DisplaceStoneSubdiv} w=3/5 — cot(phi/2) is the BISECTOR partition requirement, not every edge requirement. Sizing a SMOOTH seam by it (cot 5 deg = 11.4) replaced the curved wall with its tangent planes extended a whole unit, each strip standing proud of the curve by the sagitta and the pair mutually satisfying each other mitre trim. A crease is a corner and needs max(cot,tan)(phi/2); a smooth seam is one surface and needs only the mitre tan(phi/2). Max margin 1.059 -> 0.578 u.
- `80a9fd84d55d` trap **greets.displace.v4.trap.flip_rate_instrument** = "instrument_blind_to_v4" (state) {detector=flip_rate scene=greets state=P3b tool=flip_rate} w=3/5 — tools/flip_rate.sh CANNOT measure a v4 arm: its greets recipe omits --greets-displace, so --greets_displace_v4 is inert and 24 runs reproduce the OFF-arm pin no matter what arm flags you append
- `ba7c2e825b1f` trap **greets.render.mip_is_per_polygon** = 265108 (count) {granularity=whole_frame metric=miplevel scene=greets} w=3/5 — THE ALBEDO MIP IS A FUNCTION OF THE TESSELLATION, NOT ONLY OF THE CAMERA. FDS/FRUSTRUM/FRUSTRUM.CPP:945 picks the miplevel per CLIPPED POLYGON: under MinSize = XRes*YRes*0.02 pixels (41472 at 1920x1080) one mip is taken for the whole polygon from 0.5*log2(texArea*SizeX*SizeY/pixArea); above it the mipmap-via-subdivision path splits the polygon and each piece gets its own. So subdividing a wall changes which mip its pixels sample even when every new vertex lies exactly on the authored plane: 265108 pixels change mip (0->1 on 225884, 0->2 on 39224) between the 226 authored stone triangles and a 60606-triangle UNDISPLACED lattice of the same planes at greets cam A, and the SHIPPED bake at amplitude 0 shows the same signature. Consequence for any 'byte-identical after retessellation' plan: it cannot hold. Consequence for any A/B whose arms differ in face count: the colour delta is not attributable to the shading change under test until the mip plane is differenced -- the G-buffer packs it in bits 28..31 of the mat32 word, dumpable with FDS_SNAPSHOT_GBUFDUMP and read by tools/v4_p2_planes.py.
- `015ae8c8e985` trap **measure.concurrent_merge_contaminated_arm** = "trap" (state) {state=rwdi_test_2026_08_30 tool=m5} w=1/5 — The dev-box RelWithDebInfo ceiling test was CONTAMINATED by my own concurrent merge: the GTAO-defaults merge landed on fog-wt between the Release reference render and the RWDI build, so the RWDI arm silently carried the NEW AO defaults and the 96%-of-pixels difference measured the flag flip, not -O2. The only valid comparison from that batch remains M5 vs the pre-merge local Release (24.79%, the ceiling block). Rule: an A/B across builds must pin the SOURCE SHA of both arms (detached worktree at a fixed commit), never build from a branch another task is advancing. The provenance sidecars carry the SHA - read them before diffing.
- `94716a6a34db` trap **measure.min_of_n_interleaved_with_duplicate_arm** = "trap" (exact) {} w=1/5  ‹global› — Perf A/B: interleave the arms, rotate order, take the min of N (11), and run a DUPLICATE of one arm as the contamination detector - a between-arm delta smaller than the duplicate's drift is not a finding. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from revival-fog]
- `acb4a44152f1` trap **measure.printf_hides_races** = "trap" (exact) {} w=1/5  ‹global› — printf/stderr in a hot threaded path serialises the workers and HIDES the race you are hunting (heisenbug); record lock-free per-worker and dump at the end, or build-bisect. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from revival-fog]
- `948796f13635` trap **measure.two_run_md5_proves_nothing** = "trap" (exact) {} w=1/5  ‹global› — A 2-run byte-identical gate cannot clear a threading race; determinism needs 24+ identical runs (md5 | sort | uniq -c) and a binomial before believing a clean batch (P(24 clean at 1-in-12) ~ 0.12). [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from revival-fog]
- `8f04128dc038` law **perf.floor** = 2.0 (pct_delta) {granularity=kernel_row metric=delta_pct} w=5/5 — a single kernel row's between-binary delta carries a +-1.5-2 % code-placement floor: 24 bytes of inert padding moved greets lighting-w1 by 1.21 %, and an arm with the parent's exact 5359-instruction kernel still carried +1.08 % (00w).
- `d5f079501e3c` law **perf.law.L1_fanout_threshold** = "holds" (state) {} w=5/5 — A fan-out pays only above a work-per-dispatch threshold of ~tens of microseconds; under ~100 us the round trip is the cost (city glass -24 %, RTT cone pass +139 %, mirror-mask clear +33 %). [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `95a401b2efdd` law **perf.law.L2_scalar_ilp** = "holds" (state) {} w=5/5 — An 8-iteration independent scalar loop is not automatically improved by vectorising it: the scalar form already extracts ILP; price it in cycles, never in instructions (SSAO gather -9.8 % instr, +2.5 % cycles). [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `2a4700fe068d` law **perf.law.L3_register_pressure** = "holds" (state) {granularity=kernel_row} w=5/5 — Register pressure beats op count in the cone and lighting kernels: C6 removed 10 vector-ALU ops and paid 29 spills, +5.42 % cycles; one __m256 is two of arm64's 32 v-registers. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `35721b7adc41` law **perf.law.L4_issue_bound** = "holds" (state) {} w=5/5 — Once a kernel is issue-bound instruction counts stop predicting time; the cone pass at ~81 % of NEON issue converts instructions to wall, lighting-w1 does not (-2.0 % instr -> +0.7..1.4 % cycles). [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `3c27392e58f7` law **perf.law.L5_cores_triage** = "holds" (state) {} w=1/5 — cores = Gcyc / clock / wall finds both the wins and the refutations: a row far below the worker count is serial or barrier-bound, one at the worker count is compute-bound. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `0236fb850a7c` law **perf.law.L6_fewer_pairs** = "holds" (state) {} w=5/5 — The only lever with real headroom on a saturated per-pixel row is FEWER PAIRS: micro-optimising the body is exhausted (cone pass -52.6 % by range cull; greets lighting-w1 8.40 lights/px enter, 2.06-3.40 accumulate). [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `e55e19b2c7a2` law **perf.law.L7_share_source** = "holds" (state) {} w=5/5 — Bit-exactness is won by SHARING SOURCE, not transcribing: the froxel reflection leg went 8-wide bit-exact first build by lifting the leg into one static inline helper both paths call. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `4a4c2a5695d3` law **perf.law.L8_per_tile_decidable** = "holds" (state) {} w=1/5 — Anything that runs per-pixel and is decidable per-tile or per-light is the first place to look, and the guard usually already exists - check before proposing it. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `6761cfcf6bae` law **perf.law.L9_runtime_predicate** = "holds" (state) {row=lighting-w1 scene=greets} w=5/5 — In a kernel at its register-allocation limit a new runtime predicate costs more than the work it removes: a never-taken skip inside the cube tap cost +12.4 %, a runtime bool hatch +4.3 % with the flag OFF; the cube tap only gets cheaper by being CALLED less. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `354922e406f8` trap **perf.trap.T10_repetition_is_blind** = "trap" (state) {tool=snapshot} w=1/5 — 24 repetitions of a blind test is still a blind test: the bsWorld regression passed 13/13 pins and 24 one-tick runs; the defect began at tick 4. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `d61bdb9768ad` trap **perf.trap.T11_half_gated_flag** = "trap" (state) {} w=1/5 — A flag that gates half a change is not a revert arm: round 5's glass change shipped a fan-out and a bsWorld cache under one flag that did not gate the cache. If --no-<flag> is not byte-identical to the parent, it is not a control. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `0ec89812ec0b` trap **perf.trap.T12_not_yours_to_rebaseline** = "trap" (state) {tool=warm_gate.sh} w=1/5 — A warm-gate red from someone else's change is not yours to re-baseline: one pixel, max 1-2, deterministic 3/3 is exactly what a stale cached bounding sphere looks like. Report to the owner, leave the baseline. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `53507365af95` trap **perf.trap.T13_lto_layout_floor** = "trap" (state) {granularity=binary} w=1/5 — Between-binary comparisons carry an LTO-layout floor of +-0.9 % on renderFrame and up to 6 % on a row; prefer same-binary flag flips, else diff per-function instruction counts to prove locality. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `48a5fe799eae` trap **perf.trap.T14_instruments_need_frames** = "trap" (state) {tool=census} w=1/5 — Instruments need the frames they were built for: both shadow censuses report every 8 main-view frames and the cone/fog censuses report the PREVIOUS frame; a one-pose snapshot prints nothing. Use --bench or a multi-pose snapshot and read the second report. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `8edd0370e6fc` trap **perf.trap.T1_green_pins_not_exercised** = "trap" (state) {tool=snapshot} w=1/5 — A green 13/13 does not mean a path was exercised: every pin is a one-tick --snapshot and default-ON paths that switch on from tick 2 execute zero times; a binary computing no city water fog passed 12/12 pins and failed 5/7 warm rows. Use tools/warm_gate.sh. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `f13a58dcf4e6` trap **perf.trap.T1b_cold_census_misprices** = "trap" (state) {tool=census} w=1/5 — A cold one-tick census mis-prices the backlog: the froxel composite's scalar half was on file at 0.72 % of renderFrame (cold, 1512x848) and is 5.15 % warm at 1920x1080, 7x low. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `94f29f379cf6` trap **perf.trap.T2_stale_shares** = "trap" (state) {scene=greets} w=1/5 — Stale shares get stale: re-take the measurement before sizing a plan off it (the 36.6 % cube-tap share stood for eleven rounds; all shadow work is 16-28 %). [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `58dd332647a8` trap **perf.trap.T3_hwread_process_wide** = "trap" (state) {tool=TailProf} w=1/5 — TailProf::hwRead() is proc_pid_rusage(getpid()) - PROCESS-WIDE; a nested scope inside a threaded pass measures every thread. Split threaded sub-scopes by ablation differencing, never by nesting a timer. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `d5d5ac74d34d` trap **perf.trap.T4_chdir_asset_root** = "trap" (state) {tool=DEMO} w=1/5 — ChdirToAssetRoot makes a binary's FILE LOCATION select its assets: DEMO chdirs to its own directory, so a worktree binary always renders that worktree's assets. Three false alarms traced to this; gate with --no-chdir_assets. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `76b2bfb5c3e4` trap **perf.trap.T5_chase_pose_sequence** = "trap" (state) {scene=chase tool=snapshot} w=1/5 — chase pins are POSE-SEQUENCE dependent: t=800 alone differs from t=800 third of five by 434 591 px (20.96 %, max 5); only the first pose of a process is comparable across recipes. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `69fbcb8b6628` trap **perf.trap.T6_pgrep_self_match** = "trap" (state) {tool=pgrep} w=1/5 — pgrep -f matches your own shell: two agents' box-quiet loops deadlocked on each other's pgrep command lines. Use ps -Ao comm= | grep -c '/DEMO$'. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `e088f40341b3` trap **perf.trap.T7_ssao_dump_inflates** = "trap" (state) {flag=ssao_dump} w=1/5 — --ssao_dump forces the scalar apply loop and inflates the pass it measures ~3.5x (4.2 -> 13.7-16.0 ms); a correctness instrument, never a perf one. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `e3623046e07e` trap **perf.trap.T8_cmake_install_rpath** = "trap" (state) {tool=cmake} w=1/5 — cmake --install fails on a pre-existing rpath step (install_name_tool: no LC_RPATH) - that is NOT a failed build; the binary is placed by the POST_BUILD copy. Verify with --no-chdir_assets. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]
- `419d92354f90` trap **perf.trap.T9_warm_gate_zero_frames** = "trap" (state) {tool=warm_gate.sh} w=1/5 — A row that emitted no frames must not print as a pixel mismatch: warm_gate.sh printed an empty got under FAIL and sent a reader hunting a regression for an hour; it now prints ERROR <row> produced N/M frames. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from docs/PERF_LAWS.md]

[seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from rev-dispfix handoff / SESSION_STATE]
- `c50b39a206fc` trap **perf.trap.tear_battery_absolute_outroot** = "trap" (state) {tool=tear_battery.sh} w=1/5 — tools/tear_battery.sh needs an ABSOLUTE outroot, or every pose silently renders nothing while printing done. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from rev-dispfix handoff / SESSION_STATE]
- `f4343b294f7e` trap **perf.trap.zsh_flag_string_splitting** = "trap" (state) {tool=zsh} w=1/5 — zsh does not word-split a variable holding a flag string: use ${=VAR} to split it, and never echo a line of ===. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from rev-dispfix handoff / SESSION_STATE]
- `31d9206979ca` trap **shell.zsh.flag_string_splitting** = "trap" (exact) {shell=zsh} w=1/5  ‹global› — zsh does not word-split an unquoted variable: a flag string in $VAR is one argument; use ${=VAR}. Also never echo a bare line of === in a script that feeds a parser. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from revival-fog]
- `ccd53bb80c90` trap **tool.cmake.language_cxx_on_mm** = "trap" (state) {tool=cmake} w=1/5 — set_source_files_properties(... LANGUAGE CXX) on Objective-C++ .mm sources emits an explicit -x c++ since CMake CMP0119 (>=3.20; NEW by default with a fresh 4.x cache), overriding the .mm extension: clang warns argument unused for -ObjC++ and then rejects every @class in the Foundation headers. Bit the M5 (fresh CLT + fresh cache) while the dev box coasted on an old cache. Fix: enable_language(OBJCXX) in the gated subdirectory and drop the LANGUAGE override; -fobjc-arc stays as a per-file option. Verified: GpuBench configures and links on the dev box with the fix.
- `1c446f7c1625` trap **tool.groundwork.amend_orphans_head** = "trap" (state) {tool=groundwork} w=1/5 — Records auto-capture the repo HEAD at write time. Writing records and then AMENDING (or rebasing away) that commit leaves their head pointing at a commit that is no longer an ancestor of the tip, so the tool treats them as unmerged-branch records: they can neither re-verify anything nor count as at-tip. Happened 2026-08-30: four agreeing render_gate rows written at f02e31b4, commit amended to 5381c68b, the four baselines stayed unverified (36 instead of 32). Rule: write records, then make a NEW commit; never amend/rebase a commit that records were written against without re-filing them.
- `3fccae10f485` trap **tool.pgrep.self_match** = "trap" (exact) {tool=pgrep} w=1/5  ‹global› — pgrep -f matches the shell that runs it: box-quiet wait loops that grep their own command line deadlock on each other. Match executables (ps -Ao comm=), not argv. [seeded 2026-08-29 by the coordinator from revival-fog]
- `609d12df45a9` trap **trap.miplevel_minsize_frozen_static** = 5242.88 (count) {metric=mip_minsize pose=camA_t5965 scene=greets tool=m5} w=3/5 — MiplevelClipper (FDS/FRUSTRUM/FRUSTRUM.CPP) computes its small-face threshold as `static const float MinSize = XRes*YRes * 0.02;` - a FUNCTION-LOCAL static, initialised ONCE by whichever thread calls first, from whatever the XRes/YRes globals held then. MEASURED at greets cam A on a 1920x1080 run: MinSize = 5242.88, i.e. 512x512 - an offscreen bake got there first, so the MAIN view runs the whole process with a threshold 7.9x too small (it should be 41472). The source comment ("update if XRes and/or YRes changes...") documents the bug and nothing does it. Consequences: the poly-split path is entered for faces meant to take the uniform-mip fast path, the value silently depends on WHICH pass touches the clipper first (a scheduling property), and a window resize never updates it. NOT the cause of the M5 divergence - both branches still rasterise the face, verified - but a live resolution-dependence trap for anyone reading mip behaviour or pricing the split path.
- `5fc35421cbaf` trap **zsh.batch_script.silent_no_op** = "trap" (exact) {project=revival-fog shell=zsh tool=zsh} w=2/5  ‹global› — Two zsh idioms turn a batch script into a silent no-op. (1) rm -f a/*.x b/*.y aborts on NOMATCH before deleting ANYTHING when one pattern matches nothing, so a self-cleaning run keeps every file - setopt local_options null_glob. (2) An option string expanded as ${OPT} instead of ${=OPT} is passed as ONE argv word, so --bare /path reaches argparse as a single unrecognised argument and every invocation fails. Both were found only because the output tree was 1681 MiB instead of 250 and every score file held a usage message, not because anything errored.
